+181|class StorageEngine {
+182|  constructor(db) {
+183|    this.db = db;
+184|  }
+185|
+186|  getUsers() {
+187|    return this.db.prepare('SELECT id, name, color FROM users').all();
+188|  }
+189|
+190|  getUser(userId) {
+191|    return this.db.prepare('SELECT id, name, color FROM users WHERE id = 
?').get(userId);
+192|  }
+193|
+194|  getWorkbook(workbookId) {
+195|    const wb = this.db.prepare('SELECT id, title, current_revision, 
created_at, updated_at FROM workbooks WHERE id = ?').get(workbookId);
+196|    if (!wb) return null;
+197|
+198|    const sheets = this.db.prepare('SELECT id, name, order_index FROM 
sheets WHERE workbook_id = ? ORDER BY order_index ASC').all(workbookId);
+199|    const cells = this.db.prepare('SELECT sheet_id, cell_ref, raw_value, 
updated_at, updated_by FROM cells WHERE workbook_id = ?').all(workbookId);
+200|
+201|    const sheetMap = {};
+202|    sheets.forEach(s => {
+203|      sheetMap[s.id] = {
+204|        id: s.id,
+205|        name: s.name,
+206|        cells: {}
+207|      };
+208|    });
+209|
+210|    cells.forEach(c => {
+211|      if (sheetMap[c.sheet_id]) {
+212|        sheetMap[c.sheet_id].cells[c.cell_ref] = c.raw_value;
+213|      }
+214|    });
+215|
+216|    return {
+217|      id: wb.id,
+218|      title: wb.title,
+219|      current_revision: wb.current_revision,
+220|      created_at: wb.created_at,
+221|      updated_at: wb.updated_at,
+222|      sheets: sheets.map(s => sheetMap[s.id]),
+223|      users: this.getUsers()
+224|    };
+225|  }
+226|
+227|  getWorkbookSnapshot(workbookId) {
+228|    const wb = this.getWorkbook(workbookId);
+229|    if (!wb) return null;
+230|    return {
+231|      id: wb.id,
+232|      title: wb.title,
+233|      sheets: wb.sheets.map(s => ({
+234|        id: s.id,
+235|        name: s.name,
+236|        cells: { ...s.cells }
+237|      }))
+238|    };
+239|  }
+240|
+241|  getRevisions(workbookId) {
+242|    return this.db.prepare(`
+243|      SELECT revision_number, user_id, user_name, description, created_at 
+244|      FROM revisions 
+245|      WHERE workbook_id = ? 
+246|      ORDER BY revision_number DESC
+247|    `).all(workbookId);
+248|  }
+249|
+250|  getRevision(workbookId, revisionNumber) {
+251|    const row = this.db.prepare(`
+252|      SELECT revision_number, user_id, user_name, description, snapshot, 
created_at 
+253|      FROM revisions 
+254|      WHERE workbook_id = ? AND revision_number = ?
+255|    `).get(workbookId, revisionNumber);
+256|    if (!row) return null;
+257|    return {
+258|      revision_number: row.revision_number,
+259|      user_id: row.user_id,
+260|      user_name: row.user_name,
+261|      description: row.description,
+262|      snapshot: JSON.parse(row.snapshot),
+263|      created_at: row.created_at
+264|    };
+265|  }
+266|
+267|  getCellHistory(workbookId, sheetId, cellRef) {
+268|    return this.db.prepare(`
+269|      SELECT id, workbook_id, sheet_id, cell_ref, revision_number, 
old_value, new_value, user_id, user_name, created_at 
+270|      FROM cell_history 
+271|      WHERE workbook_id = ? AND sheet_id = ? AND cell_ref = ?
+272|      ORDER BY revision_number DESC, id DESC
+273|    `).all(workbookId, sheetId, cellRef.toUpperCase());
+274|  }
+275|
+276|  saveWorkbook(workbookId, baseRevision, snapshot, userId, userName, 
description = 'Update') {
+277|    // 1. Validation checks
+278|    if (!Number.isInteger(baseRevision) || baseRevision < 1) {
+279|      return { success: false, error: 'Invalid base revision: must be a 
positive integer', statusCode: 400 };
+280|    }
+281|    if (!snapshot || typeof snapshot !== 'object') {
+282|      return { success: false, error: 'Malformed snapshot: must be an 
object', statusCode: 400 };
+283|    }
+284|    if (snapshot.id !== workbookId) {
+285|      return { success: false, error: `Mismatched workbook ID: expected 
${workbookId}, got ${snapshot.id}`, statusCode: 400 };
+286|    }
+287|    if (!Array.isArray(snapshot.sheets) || snapshot.sheets.length === 0) {
+288|      return { success: false, error: 'Workbook must have at least one 
sheet', statusCode: 400 };
+289|    }
+290|
+291|    const currentWb = this.getWorkbook(workbookId);
+292|    if (!currentWb) {
+293|      return { success: false, error: 'Workbook not found', statusCode: 404
};
+294|    }
+295|
+296|    if (snapshot.title !== currentWb.title) {
+297|      return { success: false, error: 'Cannot rename workbook in cell edit 
save', statusCode: 400 };
+298|    }
+299|
+300|    // Validate sheets
+301|    const currentSheetMap = {};
+302|    currentWb.sheets.forEach(s => { currentSheetMap[s.id] = s; });
+303|
+304|    for (const sheet of snapshot.sheets) {
+305|      if (!sheet.id || !currentSheetMap[sheet.id]) {
+306|        return { success: false, error: `Unknown sheet identity: 
${sheet.id}`, statusCode: 400 };
+307|      }
+308|      if (sheet.name !== currentSheetMap[sheet.id].name) {
+309|        return { success: false, error: `Cannot rename sheet ${sheet.id} in
cell edit save`, statusCode: 400 };
+310|      }
+311|      if (!sheet.cells || typeof sheet.cells !== 'object' || 
Array.isArray(sheet.cells)) {
+312|        return { success: false, error: `Malformed cells object for sheet 
${sheet.id}`, statusCode: 400 };
+313|      }
+314|      // Check every cell value is a string
+315|      for (const [ref, val] of Object.entries(sheet.cells)) {
+316|        if (typeof val !== 'string') {
+317|          return { success: false, error: `Cell ${ref} value must be a 
string, got ${typeof val}`, statusCode: 400 };
+318|        }
+319|        if (!/^[A-Za-z]+[1-9][0-9]*$/.test(ref)) {
+320|          return { success: false, error: `Invalid cell reference: ${ref}`,
statusCode: 400 };
+321|        }
+322|      }
+323|    }
+324|
+325|    const currentRev = currentWb.current_revision;
+326|
+327|    if (baseRevision > currentRev) {
+328|      return { success: false, error: `Base revision ${baseRevision} is in 
the future (current is ${currentRev})`, statusCode: 400 };
+329|    }
+330|
+331|    // Normalize snapshot cells to uppercase references
+332|    const normalizedSnapshot = {
+333|      id: snapshot.id,
+334|      title: snapshot.title,
+335|      sheets: snapshot.sheets.map(s => {
+336|        const normCells = {};
+337|        for (const [ref, val] of Object.entries(s.cells)) {
+338|          if (val !== '') {
+339|            normCells[ref.toUpperCase()] = val;
+340|          }
+341|        }
+342|        return {
+343|          id: s.id,
+344|          name: s.name,
+345|          cells: normCells
+346|        };
+347|      })
+348|    };
+349|
+350|    // Calculate diff between incoming snapshot and current state
+351|    const incomingSheet = normalizedSnapshot.sheets[0];
+352|    const currentSheet = currentWb.sheets.find(s => s.id === 
incomingSheet.id) || currentWb.sheets[0];
+353|
+354|    // Case 1: baseRevision === currentRev
+355|    if (baseRevision === currentRev) {
+356|      // Find diff between current state and incoming snapshot
+357|      const cellDiff = this._computeCellDiff(currentSheet.cells, 
incomingSheet.cells);
+358|      if (cellDiff.length === 0) {
+359|        // No changes, no new revision needed
+360|        return {
+361|          success: true,
+362|          revision: currentRev,
+363|          workbook: this.getWorkbookSnapshot(workbookId),
+364|          noChange: true
+365|        };
+366|      }
+367|
+368|      // Apply changes and create next revision
+369|      const nextRev = currentRev + 1;
+370|      const now = new Date().toISOString();
+371|
+372|      const saveTx = this.db.transaction(() => {
+373|        this._applyCellDiff(workbookId, incomingSheet.id, cellDiff, 
nextRev, userId, userName, now);
+374|        this.db.prepare('UPDATE workbooks SET current_revision = ?, 
updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?')
+375|          .run(nextRev, now, workbookId);
+376|        this.db.prepare(`
+377|          INSERT INTO revisions (workbook_id, revision_number, user_id, 
user_name, description, snapshot, created_at)
+378|          VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
+379|        `).run(workbookId, nextRev, userId, userName, description, 
JSON.stringify(normalizedSnapshot), now);
+380|      });
+381|
+382|      saveTx();
+383|
+384|      return {
+385|        success: true,
+386|        revision: nextRev,
+387|        workbook: normalizedSnapshot,
+388|        changes: cellDiff
+389|      };
+390|    }
+391|
+392|    // Case 2: baseRevision < currentRev (Concurrent save)
+393|    // Fetch snapshot at baseRevision
+394|    const baseRevRecord = this.getRevision(workbookId, baseRevision);
+395|    if (!baseRevRecord) {
+396|      return { success: false, error: `Base revision ${baseRevision} not 
found in history`, statusCode: 400 };
+397|    }
+398|
+399|    const baseSnapshot = baseRevRecord.snapshot;
+400|    const baseSheet = baseSnapshot.sheets.find(s => s.id === 
incomingSheet.id) || baseSnapshot.sheets[0];
+401|
+402|    // Find which cells the incoming save changed compared to baseRevision
+403|    const incomingChangedCells = new Set();
+404|    const allBaseKeys = new Set([...Object.keys(baseSheet.cells || {}), 
...Object.keys(incomingSheet.cells || {})]);
+405|    for (const key of allBaseKeys) {
+406|      const baseVal = (baseSheet.cells && baseSheet.cells[key]) || '';
+407|      const incVal = (incomingSheet.cells && incomingSheet.cells[key]) || 
'';
+408|      if (baseVal !== incVal) {
+409|        incomingChangedCells.add(key);
+410|      }
+411|    }
+412|
+413|    // Find which cells changed on server since baseRevision (in revisions 
baseRevision+1 .. currentRev)
+414|    const serverChanges = this.db.prepare(`
+415|      SELECT DISTINCT cell_ref 
+416|      FROM cell_history 
+417|      WHERE workbook_id = ? AND sheet_id = ? AND revision_number > ? AND 
revision_number <= ?
+418|    `).all(workbookId, incomingSheet.id, baseRevision, currentRev);
+419|
+420|    const serverChangedCells = new Set(serverChanges.map(r => r.cell_ref));
+421|
+422|    // Check for overlapping conflicting cells
+423|    const conflicts = [];
+424|    for (const cell of incomingChangedCells) {
+425|      if (serverChangedCells.has(cell)) {
+426|        conflicts.push(cell);
+427|      }
+428|    }
+429|
+430|    if (conflicts.length > 0) {
+431|      // Reject overlapping stale save
+432|      return {
+433|        success: false,
+434|        conflict: true,
+435|        conflictingCells: conflicts,
+436|        currentRevision: currentRev,
+437|        error: `Conflict: Stale save overlaps with changes made in 
revisions ${baseRevision + 1} to ${currentRev} on cell(s): ${conflicts.join(', 
')}`,
+438|        statusCode: 409
+439|      };
+440|    }
+441|
+442|    // Non-overlapping changes! Perform 3-way merge onto current state
+443|    const mergedCells = { ...currentSheet.cells };
+444|    const cellDiff = [];
+445|
+446|    for (const cell of incomingChangedCells) {
+447|      const oldVal = mergedCells[cell] || '';
+448|      const newVal = incomingSheet.cells[cell] || '';
+449|      if (newVal === '') {
+450|        delete mergedCells[cell];
+451|      } else {
+452|        mergedCells[cell] = newVal;
+453|      }
+454|      cellDiff.push({ cell, oldVal, newVal });
+455|    }
+456|
+457|    const mergedSnapshot = {
+458|      id: workbookId,
+459|      title: currentWb.title,
+460|      sheets: [
+461|        {
+462|          id: incomingSheet.id,
+463|          name: incomingSheet.name,
+464|          cells: mergedCells
+465|        }
+466|      ]
+467|    };
+468|
+469|    const nextRev = currentRev + 1;
+470|    const now = new Date().toISOString();
+471|
+472|    const mergeTx = this.db.transaction(() => {
+473|      this._applyCellDiff(workbookId, incomingSheet.id, cellDiff, nextRev, 
userId, userName, now);
+474|      this.db.prepare('UPDATE workbooks SET current_revision = ?, 
updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?')
+475|        .run(nextRev, now, workbookId);
+476|      this.db.prepare(`
+477|        INSERT INTO revisions (workbook_id, revision_number, user_id, 
user_name, description, snapshot, created_at)
+478|        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
+479|      `).run(workbookId, nextRev, userId, userName, description + ' 
(Merged)', JSON.stringify(mergedSnapshot), now);
+480|    });
+481|
+482|    mergeTx();
+483|
+484|    return {
+485|      success: true,
+486|      revision: nextRev,
+487|      workbook: mergedSnapshot,
+488|      changes: cellDiff,
+489|      merged: true
+490|    };
+491|  }
+492|
+493|  _computeCellDiff(currentCells, incomingCells) {
+494|    const diff = [];
+495|    const allKeys = new Set([...Object.keys(currentCells || {}), 
...Object.keys(incomingCells || {})]);
+496|    for (const key of allKeys) {
+497|      const currVal = (currentCells && currentCells[key]) || '';
+498|      const incVal = (incomingCells && incomingCells[key]) || '';
+499|      if (currVal !== incVal) {
+500|        diff.push({ cell: key, oldVal: currVal, newVal: incVal });
+501|      }
+502|    }
+503|    return diff;
+504|  }
+505|
+506|  _applyCellDiff(workbookId, sheetId, diff, revisionNumber, userId, 
userName, timestamp) {
+507|    const insertCell = this.db.prepare('INSERT OR REPLACE INTO cells 
(workbook_id, sheet_id, cell_ref, raw_value, updated_at, updated_by) VALUES (?, 
?, ?, ?, ?, ?)');
+508|    const deleteCell = this.db.prepare('DELETE FROM cells WHERE workbook_id
= ? AND sheet_id = ? AND cell_ref = ?');
+509|    const insertCellHistory = this.db.prepare(`
+510|      INSERT INTO cell_history (workbook_id, sheet_id, cell_ref, 
revision_number, old_value, new_value, user_id, user_name, created_at)
+511|      VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
+512|    `);
+513|
+514|    for (const change of diff) {
+515|      if (change.newVal === '') {
+516|        deleteCell.run(workbookId, sheetId, change.cell);
+517|      } else {
+518|        insertCell.run(workbookId, sheetId, change.cell, change.newVal, 
timestamp, userId);
+519|      }
+520|      insertCellHistory.run(workbookId, sheetId, change.cell, 
revisionNumber, change.oldVal || null, change.newVal, userId, userName, 
timestamp);
+521|    }
+522|  }
+523|}

Summary: Create Express REST API router

Reasoning:
**API Design and Real-time Collaboration - Time to Get Down to Business!**

Okay, the database schema is up and running, initialization is done. Task 2 is 
officially in the rearview mirror. Time to tackle Task 3: the Server API and 
that real-time collaboration engine. This is where things get interesting!

First, the API endpoints. I need to design the REST and WebSocket routes 
meticulously. Let's start with the REST API. I'll need to reference 
`overview.md`, `storage.md`, `collaboration.md`, and `interface.md` as I go to 
make sure I don't miss anything.

For getting workbook data, I'm thinking:

*   `GET /api/workbook` or `GET /api/workbooks/:id` should provide the current 
workbook data. `GET /api/workbook` can default to "ops-plan". The response will 
be a JSON object with the workbook's ID, title, current revision, sheets (with 
cell data), and users.

For saving the workbook, the options seem to be:

*   `POST /api/workbooks/:id/save`, `PUT /api/workbooks/:id` or `POST 
/api/workbooks/:id/revisions`

Given the requirements, each save needs to include the workbook identity, a 
complete snapshot of the workbook, and the revision number it's based on. The 
manifest's details will guide us here too. I'll support both `GET 
/api/workbooks/:id` (for getting the workbook), and `PUT /api/workbooks/:id` and
`POST /api/workbooks/:id/save` (for saving). The request body will include the 
workbook's ID (or snapshot ID), base revision, the full workbook data, user 
information, and a description.

I also need to support getting revisions (`GET /api/workbooks/:id/revisions`, 
`GET /api/workbooks/:id/revisions/:revisionNumber`), cell history (`GET 
/api/workbooks/:id/sheets/:sheetId/cells/:cellRef/history`), users (`GET 
/api/users`), and active user presence (`GET /api/presence` or via WebSocket).

Now, let's switch gears to the WebSocket collaboration protocol.

*   The URL will be `/ws`.
*   Clients will connect and join with a `join` message that includes their 
session ID, user ID, user name, and workbook ID.
*   Presence updates (`presence` messages) will be sent whenever a user moves or
selects cells, including the cell range.
*   User switches will be communicated with a `switch_user` message.
*   The server will broadcast a presence map (`presence_update` messages) to all
connected clients, including session ID, user info, color, cell, range, and last
active timestamp.
*   Crucially, when a save happens (either REST or WebSocket), the server needs 
to broadcast a `revision_saved` message to notify everyone.

I need to make sure the client can save both via the REST API (`POST 
/api/workbooks/:id/save` and `PUT /api/workbooks/:id`) AND via WebSocket message
`{ type: "save", ... }`, ensuring consistency across the two.

Error handling is critical. I'll need to handle:

*   Malformed data: Return a 400 Bad Request with a descriptive JSON error.
*   Overlapping conflicts (stale saves): Return a 409 Conflict with a detailed 
error message and a list of conflicting cells.
*   Non-overlapping concurrent saves: I'll implement a 3-way merge and return a 
200 OK with the merged snapshot and `merged: true`.

Time to put it into code! I'll start with `/app/server/routes.js`, 
`/app/server/ws.js`, and `/app/server/index.js`.

    const express = require('express');
    
    function createApiRouter(storageEngine, wsManager) {
      const router = express.Router();
      router.use(express.json({ limit: '10mb' }));
    
      // GET /api/users
      router.get('/users', (req, res) => {
        try {
          const users = storageEngine.getUsers();
          res.json(users);
        } catch (err) {
          res.status(500).json({ error: err.message });
        }
      });
    
      // GET /api/workbook (default ops-plan)
      router.get('/workbook', (req, res) => {
        try {
          const wb = storageEngine.getWorkbook('ops-plan');
          if (!wb) return res.status(404).json({ error: 'Workbook not found' });
          res.json(wb);
        } catch (err) {
          res.status(500).json({ error: err.message });
        }
      });
    
      // GET /api/workbooks/:id
      router.get('/workbooks/:id', (req, res) => {
        try {
          const wb = storageEngine.getWorkbook(req.params.id);
          if (!wb) return res.status(404).json({ error: 'Workbook not found' });
          res.json(wb);
        } catch (err) {
          res.status(500).json({ error: err.message });
        }
      });
    
      // Save handler for both PUT /api/workbooks/:id and POST 
/api/workbooks/:id/save
      const handleSave = (req, res) => {
        try {
          const workbookId = req.params.id;
          const { baseRevision, workbook, userId, userName, description } = 
req.body;
    
          if (baseRevision === undefined || baseRevision === null) {
            return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Missing baseRevision in 
request' });
          }
          if (!workbook) {
            return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Missing workbook snapshot in 
request' });
          }
    
          // Check user
          let authorId = userId || 'anonymous';
          let authorName = userName || 'Anonymous';
          if (userId) {
            const user = storageEngine.getUser(userId);
            if (user) {
              authorName = user.name;
            }
          }
    
          const result = storageEngine.saveWorkbook(
            workbookId,
            baseRevision,
            workbook,
            authorId,
            authorName,
            description || 'Save'
          );
    
          if (!result.success) {
            return res.status(result.statusCode || 400).json(result);
          }
    
          // If changes occurred, notify WebSocket clients
          if (!result.noChange && wsManager) {
            wsManager.broadcastRevisionSaved(workbookId, {
              revision: result.revision,
              workbook: result.workbook,
              user: { id: authorId, name: authorName },
              description: description || 'Save',
              merged: !!result.merged
            });
          }
    
          return res.json(result);
        } catch (err) {
          console.error('Save error:', err);
          return res.status(500).json({ error: err.message });
        }
      };
    
      router.put('/workbooks/:id', handleSave);
      router.post('/workbooks/:id/save', handleSave);
    
      // GET /api/workbooks/:id/revisions
      router.get('/workbooks/:id/revisions', (req, res) => {
        try {
          const revisions = storageEngine.getRevisions(req.params.id);
          res.json(revisions);
        } catch (err) {
          res.status(500).json({ error: err.message });
        }
      });
    
      // GET /api/workbooks/:id/revisions/:rev
      router.get('/workbooks/:id/revisions/:rev', (req, res) => {
        try {
          const revNum = parseInt(req.params.rev, 10);
          if (isNaN(revNum)) {
            return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Revision must be an integer' 
});
          }
          const revision = storageEngine.getRevision(req.params.id, revNum);
          if (!revision) {
            return res.status(404).json({ error: `Revision ${revNum} not found` 
});
          }
          res.json(revision);
        } catch (err) {
          res.status(500).json({ error: err.message });
        }
      });
    
      // GET /api/workbooks/:id/sheets/:sheetId/cells/:cellRef/history
      router.get('/workbooks/:id/sheets/:sheetId/cells/:cellRef/history', (req, 
res) => {
        try {
          const history = storageEngine.getCellHistory(
            req.params.id,
            req.params.sheetId,
            req.params.cellRef
          );
          res.json(history);
        } catch (err) {
          res.status(500).json({ error: err.message });
        }
      });
    
      return router;
    }
    
    module.exports = { createApiRouter };

Summary: Create WebSocketManager for presence and real-time broadcasts

Action: FileEditorAction

Arguments:
  command: "create"
  path: "/app/server/ws.js"
  file_text: 
    const { WebSocketServer, WebSocket } = require('ws');
    
    const PALETTE = [
      '#2563eb', // blue
      '#059669', // emerald
      '#7c3aed', // purple
      '#d97706', // amber
      '#e11d48', // rose
      '#0891b2', // cyan
      '#4f46e5', // indigo
      '#c026d3', // fuchsia
      '#ea580c', // orange
      '#16a34a', // green
    ];
    
    class WebSocketManager {
      constructor(storageEngine) {
        this.storageEngine = storageEngine;
        this.wss = null;
        this.sessions = new Map(); // sessionId -> session details
        this.clientSockets = new Map(); // ws -> sessionId
        this.colorIndex = 0;
      }
    
      attach(server) {
        this.wss = new WebSocketServer({ server, path: '/ws' });
    
        this.wss.on('connection', (ws, req) => {
          let currentSessionId = null;
    
          ws.on('message', (data) => {
            try {
              const msg = JSON.parse(data.toString());
              this.handleMessage(ws, msg);
            } catch (err) {
              console.error('Error handling WS message:', err);
              ws.send(JSON.stringify({ type: 'error', message: 'Invalid JSON 
message' }));
            }
          });
    
          ws.on('close', () => {
            const sid = this.clientSockets.get(ws);
            if (sid) {
              this.clientSockets.delete(ws);
              this.sessions.delete(sid);
              this.broadcastPresence();
            }
          });
    
          ws.on('error', (err) => {
            console.error('WS client error:', err);
          });
        });
    
        // Clean up stale sessions every 10 seconds
        setInterval(() => {
          const now = Date.now();
          let changed = false;
          for (const [sid, session] of this.sessions.entries()) {
            if (now - session.lastSeen > 30000) { // 30s timeout
              this.sessions.delete(sid);
              if (session.ws) {
                this.clientSockets.delete(session.ws);
              }
              changed = true;
            }
          }
          if (changed) {
            this.broadcastPresence();
          }
        }, 10000);
      }
    
      handleMessage(ws, msg) {
        const { type, sessionId, userId, userName, cell, range, workbookId } = 
msg;
    
        if (type === 'join') {
          if (!sessionId || typeof sessionId !== 'string') {
            ws.send(JSON.stringify({ type: 'error', message: 'Missing sessionId'
}));
            return;
          }
    
          // Check valid user
          const users = this.storageEngine.getUsers();
          const validUser = users.find(u => u.id === userId);
          const effectiveUserId = validUser ? validUser.id : (userId || 
'riley');
          const effectiveUserName = validUser ? validUser.name : (userName || 
'Riley Stone');
          const assignedColor = validUser ? validUser.color : 
PALETTE[this.colorIndex++ % PALETTE.length];
    
          const session = {
            sessionId,
            ws,
            userId: effectiveUserId,
            userName: effectiveUserName,
            color: assignedColor,
            cell: cell || 'A1',
            range: range || null,
            workbookId: workbookId || 'ops-plan',
            lastSeen: Date.now()
          };
    
          this.sessions.set(sessionId, session);
          this.clientSockets.set(ws, sessionId);
    
          // Acknowledge join
          ws.send(JSON.stringify({
            type: 'joined',
            sessionId,
            userId: effectiveUserId,
            userName: effectiveUserName,
            color: assignedColor
          }));
    
          this.broadcastPresence();
          return;
        }
    
        if (!sessionId || !this.sessions.has(sessionId)) {
          ws.send(JSON.stringify({ type: 'error', message: 'Unrecognized 
session. Please rejoin.' }));
          return;
        }
    
        const session = this.sessions.get(sessionId);
        session.lastSeen = Date.now();
    
        if (type === 'presence') {
          session.cell = cell || session.cell;
          session.range = range !== undefined ? range : session.range;
          this.broadcastPresence();
        } else if (type === 'switch_user') {
          const users = this.storageEngine.getUsers();
          const validUser = users.find(u => u.id === userId);
          if (validUser) {
            session.userId = validUser.id;
            session.userName = validUser.name;
            session.color = validUser.color || session.color;
          } else if (userId && userName) {
            session.userId = userId;
            session.userName = userName;
          }
          ws.send(JSON.stringify({
            type: 'user_switched',
            userId: session.userId,
            userName: session.userName,
            color: session.color
          }));
          this.broadcastPresence();
        } else if (type === 'ping') {
          ws.send(JSON.stringify({ type: 'pong' }));
        }
      }
    
      broadcastPresence() {
        const list = [];
        for (const session of this.sessions.values()) {
          list.push({
            sessionId: session.sessionId,
            userId: session.userId,
            userName: session.userName,
            color: session.color,
            cell: session.cell,
            range: session.range
          });
        }
    
        const payload = JSON.stringify({
          type: 'presence_update',
          sessions: list
        });
    
        for (const session of this.sessions.values()) {
          if (session.ws && session.ws.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {
            session.ws.send(payload);
          }
        }
      }
    
      broadcastRevisionSaved(workbookId, data) {
        const payload = JSON.stringify({
          type: 'revision_saved',
          workbookId,
          ...data
        });
    
        for (const session of this.sessions.values()) {
          if (session.workbookId === workbookId && session.ws && 
session.ws.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {
            session.ws.send(payload);
          }
        }
      }
    }
    
    module.exports = { WebSocketManager };
    

  kind: "FileEditorAction"

reasoning 228 (total 11.24K) • ↓ output 1.86K (total 26.9K) • $ (total 0.2322)

+1|const { WebSocketServer, WebSocket } = require('ws');
 2|
+3|const PALETTE = [
+4|  '#2563eb', // blue
+5|  '#059669', // emerald
+6|  '#7c3aed', // purple
+7|  '#d97706', // amber
+8|  '#e11d48', // rose
+9|  '#0891b2', // cyan
+10|  '#4f46e5', // indigo
+11|  '#c026d3', // fuchsia
+12|  '#ea580c', // orange
+13|  '#16a34a', // green
+14|];
+15|
+16|class WebSocketManager {
+17|  constructor(storageEngine) {
+18|    this.storageEngine = storageEngine;
+19|    this.wss = null;
+20|    this.sessions = new Map(); // sessionId -> session details
+21|    this.clientSockets = new Map(); // ws -> sessionId
+22|    this.colorIndex = 0;
+23|  }
+24|
+25|  attach(server) {
+26|    this.wss = new WebSocketServer({ server, path: '/ws' });
+27|
+28|    this.wss.on('connection', (ws, req) => {
+29|      let currentSessionId = null;
+30|
+31|      ws.on('message', (data) => {
+32|        try {
+33|          const msg = JSON.parse(data.toString());
+34|          this.handleMessage(ws, msg);
+35|        } catch (err) {
+36|          console.error('Error handling WS message:', err);
+37|          ws.send(JSON.stringify({ type: 'error', message: 'Invalid JSON 
message' }));
+38|        }
+39|      });
+40|
+41|      ws.on('close', () => {
+42|        const sid = this.clientSockets.get(ws);
+43|        if (sid) {
+44|          this.clientSockets.delete(ws);
+45|          this.sessions.delete(sid);
+46|          this.broadcastPresence();
+47|        }
+48|      });
+49|
+50|      ws.on('error', (err) => {
+51|        console.error('WS client error:', err);
+52|      });
+53|    });
+54|
+55|    // Clean up stale sessions every 10 seconds
+56|    setInterval(() => {
+57|      const now = Date.now();
+58|      let changed = false;
+59|      for (const [sid, session] of this.sessions.entries()) {
+60|        if (now - session.lastSeen > 30000) { // 30s timeout
+61|          this.sessions.delete(sid);
+62|          if (session.ws) {
+63|            this.clientSockets.delete(session.ws);
+64|          }
+65|          changed = true;
+66|        }
+67|      }
+68|      if (changed) {
+69|        this.broadcastPresence();
+70|      }
+71|    }, 10000);
+72|  }
+73|
+74|  handleMessage(ws, msg) {
+75|    const { type, sessionId, userId, userName, cell, range, workbookId } = 
msg;
+76|
+77|    if (type === 'join') {
+78|      if (!sessionId || typeof sessionId !== 'string') {
+79|        ws.send(JSON.stringify({ type: 'error', message: 'Missing sessionId'
}));
+80|        return;
+81|      }
+82|
+83|      // Check valid user
+84|      const users = this.storageEngine.getUsers();
+85|      const validUser = users.find(u => u.id === userId);
+86|      const effectiveUserId = validUser ? validUser.id : (userId || 
'riley');
+87|      const effectiveUserName = validUser ? validUser.name : (userName || 
'Riley Stone');
+88|      const assignedColor = validUser ? validUser.color : 
PALETTE[this.colorIndex++ % PALETTE.length];
+89|
+90|      const session = {
+91|        sessionId,
+92|        ws,
+93|        userId: effectiveUserId,
+94|        userName: effectiveUserName,
+95|        color: assignedColor,
+96|        cell: cell || 'A1',
+97|        range: range || null,
+98|        workbookId: workbookId || 'ops-plan',
+99|        lastSeen: Date.now()
+100|      };
+101|
+102|      this.sessions.set(sessionId, session);
+103|      this.clientSockets.set(ws, sessionId);
+104|
+105|      // Acknowledge join
+106|      ws.send(JSON.stringify({
+107|        type: 'joined',
+108|        sessionId,
+109|        userId: effectiveUserId,
+110|        userName: effectiveUserName,
+111|        color: assignedColor
+112|      }));
+113|
+114|      this.broadcastPresence();
+115|      return;
+116|    }
+117|
+118|    if (!sessionId || !this.sessions.has(sessionId)) {
+119|      ws.send(JSON.stringify({ type: 'error', message: 'Unrecognized 
session. Please rejoin.' }));
+120|      return;
+121|    }
+122|
+123|    const session = this.sessions.get(sessionId);
+124|    session.lastSeen = Date.now();
+125|
+126|    if (type === 'presence') {
+127|      session.cell = cell || session.cell;
+128|      session.range = range !== undefined ? range : session.range;
+129|      this.broadcastPresence();
+130|    } else if (type === 'switch_user') {
+131|      const users = this.storageEngine.getUsers();
+132|      const validUser = users.find(u => u.id === userId);
+133|      if (validUser) {
+134|        session.userId = validUser.id;
+135|        session.userName = validUser.name;
+136|        session.color = validUser.color || session.color;
+137|      } else if (userId && userName) {
+138|        session.userId = userId;
+139|        session.userName = userName;
+140|      }
+141|      ws.send(JSON.stringify({
+142|        type: 'user_switched',
+143|        userId: session.userId,
+144|        userName: session.userName,
+145|        color: session.color
+146|      }));
+147|      this.broadcastPresence();
+148|    } else if (type === 'ping') {
+149|      ws.send(JSON.stringify({ type: 'pong' }));
+150|    }
+151|  }
+152|
+153|  broadcastPresence() {
+154|    const list = [];
+155|    for (const session of this.sessions.values()) {
+156|      list.push({
+157|        sessionId: session.sessionId,
+158|        userId: session.userId,
+159|        userName: session.userName,
+160|        color: session.color,
+161|        cell: session.cell,
+162|        range: session.range
+163|      });
+164|    }
+165|
+166|    const payload = JSON.stringify({
+167|      type: 'presence_update',
+168|      sessions: list
+169|    });
+170|
+171|    for (const session of this.sessions.values()) {
+172|      if (session.ws && session.ws.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {
+173|        session.ws.send(payload);
+174|      }
+175|    }
+176|  }
+177|
+178|  broadcastRevisionSaved(workbookId, data) {
+179|    const payload = JSON.stringify({
+180|      type: 'revision_saved',
+181|      workbookId,
+182|      ...data
+183|    });
+184|
+185|    for (const session of this.sessions.values()) {
+186|      if (session.workbookId === workbookId && session.ws && 
session.ws.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) {
+187|        session.ws.send(payload);
+188|      }
+189|    }
+190|  }
+191|}
+192|
+193|module.exports = { WebSocketManager };

    const http = require('http');
    const path = require('path');
    const express = require('express');
    const { initDatabase, StorageEngine, DB_PATH } = require('./db');
    const { createApiRouter } = require('./routes');
    const { WebSocketManager } = require('./ws');
    
    const PORT = parseInt(process.env.PORT, 10) || 3000;
    const HOST = '0.0.0.0';
    
    // Initialize Database
    const db = initDatabase();
    const storageEngine = new StorageEngine(db);
    
    // Initialize Express App
    const app = express();
    const wsManager = new WebSocketManager(storageEngine);
    
    // Middleware
    app.use(express.json({ limit: '10mb' }));
    app.use(express.urlencoded({ extended: true }));
    
    // Serve static assets from /app/public
    app.use(express.static(path.join(__dirname, '..', 'public')));
    
    // API Routes
    app.use('/api', createApiRouter(storageEngine, wsManager));
    
    // Fallback to index.html for root or SPA
    app.get('*', (req, res) => {
      res.sendFile(path.join(__dirname, '..', 'public', 'index.html'));
    });
    
    // Create HTTP Server
    const server = http.createServer(app);
    
    // Attach WebSocket Server
    wsManager.attach(server);
    
    // Start listening
    if (require.main === module) {
      server.listen(PORT, HOST, () => {
        console.log(`GridForge server running at http://${HOST}:${PORT}`);
        console.log(`SQLite database located at: ${DB_PATH}`);
      });
    }
    
    module.exports = { app, server, storageEngine, wsManager };

Agent Action ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

Summary: Create Formula & Calculation Engine in /app/public/js/formula.js

Reasoning:
**Formula Engine Design - Deep Dive**

Okay, Task 3 is done, time to dive into Task 4: the Formula & Calculation 
Engine. First, I need to fully understand the requirements. The `spreadsheet.md`
file outlines the needs: formulas starting with `=`, arithmetic, parentheses, 
cell and range references, and specific functions like `SUM`, `AVG`, `MIN`, 
`MAX`, and `COUNT`. The user will mix everything. The engine needs to display 
calculated results while keeping the original formula for editing. Crucially, it
must update dependent cells when inputs change. Suggesting functions while 
typing and allowing easy cell referencing with the mouse are also critical. 
Error handling is paramount: invalid syntax, division by zero, and circular 
references must be handled gracefully, allowing correction and recalculation. 
Finally, the "fill" feature needs to maintain number patterns and adjust 
relative references.

Alright, let's detail the custom Formula Engine. First up, cell references. I'll
stick with the standard spreadsheet format: column letters (case-insensitive 
with support for AA, AB, etc.) and row numbers, with optional `$` for absolute 
referencing. Ranges are simple to define with a colon between start and end. 
I'll need a range expansion helper function, something like `expandRange("A1", 
"B2")` that returns a list of individual cell references within the range.

Next, I'll build the tokenizer/lexer. I'll break down the formula string into 
tokens: numbers, strings, booleans, identifiers (including the function names), 
cell and range references, operators (arithmetic, comparison, etc.), 
parentheses, and commas.

The parser will be the heart of the engine. I'll use a recursive descent parser 
or a Pratt parser. I'll handle unary and binary operators with standard 
precedence. I need to handle function calls with arguments that can be 
expressions, ranges, cells, or values. Parenthesized expressions need correct 
grouping, of course. Cell and range references must be resolved. The parser will
construct the Abstract Syntax Tree (AST).

Then the functions themselves. `SUM`, `AVG`, `MIN`, `MAX`, and `COUNT` will be 
implemented. Critically, these need to handle mixed arguments – numbers, ranges,
and cell references – and also ignore non-numeric cells in ranges (though errors
may propagate if there's explicit math on a non-numeric string). Division by 
zero in `AVG` will return `#DIV/0!`. Empty cells in a range are treated as zero 
for arithmetic. For non-numeric cells, I'll need to check the standards: 
Arithmetic with such values returns `#VALUE!`.

Now, the tricky part: dependency graphs and circular reference detection. I need
to parse *all* formulas in the sheet to build a directed graph of cell 
dependencies. If `D2` is `=B2*C2`, then `D2` depends on `B2` and `C2`. Building 
the graph is straightforward, then I'll use Tarjan's algorithm or DFS with a 
visited set to detect cycles. If a cycle is detected (like `A1 = A1 + 1`), the 
offending cells will be flagged with `#CIRCULAR!`. I'll evaluate the cells in 
topological order, non-formula cells first. Errors in input cells will propagate
to dependent cells.

Finally, reference shifting for "fill". When a formula is copied from `(row1, 
col1)` to `(row2, col2)`, I'll calculate the row and column differences. For 
each cell or range reference in the formula, I'll shift the column and row based
on the deltas. Non-absolute references will be shifted. If the shifted reference
goes out of bounds (row or column < 1), it will be replaced with `#REF!`. For 
example, `=B2*C2` filled down one row becomes `=B3*C3`, while `=$B$2*C2` filled 
down becomes `=$B$2*C3`.

I need a *really* solid test suite. And, I'll need to ensure this Formula Engine
is pure JavaScript, able to run on both the client (browser) and the server, and
especially usable in the test suite itself.

I'll start writing `/app/public/js/formula.js` to implement all this: the 
parsing, evaluation, all the different error types, the dependency graph, the 
circular reference detection, and the reference shifting. This needs to be a 
robust and maintainable module.




Action: FileEditorAction

Arguments:
  command: "create"
  path: "/app/public/js/formula.js"
  file_text: 
    /**
     * GridForge Formula & Calculation Engine
     * Pure JavaScript - zero external dependencies.
     */
    
    (function (root, factory) {
      if (typeof module === 'object' && module.exports) {
        module.exports = factory();
      } else {
        root.FormulaEngine = factory();
      }
    })(typeof self !== 'undefined' ? self : this, function () {
    
      // Helper: column letter <-> index (1-based: A=1, B=2, ..., Z=26, 
AA=27...)
      function colLetterToIndex(colStr) {
        let index = 0;
        const str = colStr.toUpperCase();
        for (let i = 0; i < str.length; i++) {
          index = index * 26 + (str.charCodeAt(i) - 64);
        }
        return index;
      }
    
      function indexToColLetter(index) {
        let col = '';
        let temp = index;
        while (temp > 0) {
          let rem = (temp - 1) % 26;
          col = String.fromCharCode(65 + rem) + col;
          temp = Math.floor((temp - 1) / 26);
        }
        return col;
      }
    
      // Parse cell reference: "A1", "$B$2", "C$3", "$D4" -> { colStr, rowNum, 
colIndex, isColAbs, isRowAbs }
      function parseCellRef(ref) {
        const match = ref.match(/^(\$?)([A-Za-z]+)(\$?)([1-9][0-9]*)$/);
        if (!match) return null;
        const isColAbs = match[1] === '$';
        const colStr = match[2].toUpperCase();
        const isRowAbs = match[3] === '$';
        const rowNum = parseInt(match[4], 10);
        const colIndex = colLetterToIndex(colStr);
        return {
          raw: ref,
          colStr,
          rowNum,
          colIndex,
          isColAbs,
          isRowAbs,
          normalized: `${colStr}${rowNum}`
        };
      }
    
      // Expand range: "A1:B3" -> ["A1", "A2", "A3", "B1", "B2", "B3"]
      function expandRange(startRef, endRef) {
        const pStart = parseCellRef(startRef);
        const pEnd = parseCellRef(endRef);
        if (!pStart || !pEnd) return [];
    
        const minCol = Math.min(pStart.colIndex, pEnd.colIndex);
        const maxCol = Math.max(pStart.colIndex, pEnd.colIndex);
        const minRow = Math.min(pStart.rowNum, pEnd.rowNum);
        const maxRow = Math.max(pStart.rowNum, pEnd.rowNum);
    
        const cells = [];
        for (let c = minCol; c <= maxCol; c++) {
          const colLetter = indexToColLetter(c);
          for (let r = minRow; r <= maxRow; r++) {
            cells.push(`${colLetter}${r}`);
          }
        }
        return cells;
      }
    
      // Shift cell reference by deltaRow and deltaCol (respecting $ absolute 
modifiers)
      function shiftCellRef(ref, deltaRow, deltaCol) {
        const p = parseCellRef(ref);
        if (!p) return ref;
    
        let targetCol = p.colIndex;
        if (!p.isColAbs) {
          targetCol += deltaCol;
        }
        let targetRow = p.rowNum;
        if (!p.isRowAbs) {
          targetRow += deltaRow;
        }
    
        if (targetCol < 1 || targetRow < 1) {
          return '#REF!';
        }
    
        const colStr = indexToColLetter(targetCol);
        const prefixCol = p.isColAbs ? '$' : '';
        const prefixRow = p.isRowAbs ? '$' : '';
        return `${prefixCol}${colStr}${prefixRow}${targetRow}`;
      }
    
      // Shift formula references
      function shiftFormula(formulaStr, deltaRow, deltaCol) {
        if (!formulaStr || !formulaStr.startsWith('=')) return formulaStr;
        const formulaBody = formulaStr.slice(1);
    
        // Replace range refs and cell refs using regex
        // Range regex: 
(\$?[A-Za-z]+\$?[1-9][0-9]*):(\$?[A-Za-z]+\$?[1-9][0-9]*)
        // Cell regex: (\$?[A-Za-z]+\$?[1-9][0-9]*)
        const rangeRegex = 
/(\$?[A-Za-z]+\$?[1-9][0-9]*):(\$?[A-Za-z]+\$?[1-9][0-9]*)/g;
        const cellRegex = 
/(^|[^A-Za-z0-9_:\$])(\$?[A-Za-z]+\$?[1-9][0-9]*)(?![A-Za-z0-9_:\(])/g;
    
        let shifted = formulaBody.replace(rangeRegex, (match, ref1, ref2) => {
          const s1 = shiftCellRef(ref1, deltaRow, deltaCol);
          const s2 = shiftCellRef(ref2, deltaRow, deltaCol);
          if (s1 === '#REF!' || s2 === '#REF!') return '#REF!';
          return `${s1}:${s2}`;
        });
    
        shifted = shifted.replace(cellRegex, (match, prefix, ref) => {
          const shiftedRef = shiftCellRef(ref, deltaRow, deltaCol);
          return `${prefix}${shiftedRef}`;
        });
    
        return `=${shifted}`;
      }
    
      // Extract all referenced cell addresses from a formula string (returns 
array of uppercase normalized cell keys)
      function extractDependencies(formulaStr) {
        if (!formulaStr || !formulaStr.startsWith('=')) return [];
        const deps = new Set();
        const str = formulaStr.slice(1);
    
        // Match ranges: A1:B10
        const rangeRegex = 
/([A-Za-z]+\$?[1-9][0-9]*):([A-Za-z]+\$?[1-9][0-9]*)/g;
        let match;
        const stripped = str.replace(rangeRegex, (m, r1, r2) => {
          const cells = expandRange(r1, r2);
          cells.forEach(c => deps.add(c));
          return '___RANGE___';
        });
    
        // Match single cell refs: A1, B2 (avoid matching function names 
followed by paren)
        const cellRegex = /\b([A-Za-z]+[1-9][0-9]*)\b(?!\s*\()/g;
        while ((match = cellRegex.exec(stripped)) !== null) {
          const ref = match[1].toUpperCase();
          // Ensure it's a valid cell ref
          if (parseCellRef(ref)) {
            deps.add(ref);
          }
        }
    
        return Array.from(deps);
      }
    
      // --- Lexer / Tokenizer ---
      const TOKEN_TYPES = {
        NUMBER: 'NUMBER',
        STRING: 'STRING',
        IDENTIFIER: 'IDENTIFIER',
        CELL_REF: 'CELL_REF',
        RANGE_REF: 'RANGE_REF',
        OPERATOR: 'OPERATOR',
        LPAREN: 'LPAREN',
        RPAREN: 'RPAREN',
        COMMA: 'COMMA',
        COLON: 'COLON'
      };
    
      function tokenize(input) {
        const tokens = [];
        let i = 0;
        const str = input.startsWith('=') ? input.slice(1) : input;
    
        while (i < str.length) {
          const char = str[i];
    
          if (/\s/.test(char)) {
            i++;
            continue;
          }
    
          if (char === '(') {
            tokens.push({ type: TOKEN_TYPES.LPAREN, value: '(' });
            i++;
            continue;
          }
          if (char === ')') {
            tokens.push({ type: TOKEN_TYPES.RPAREN, value: ')' });
            i++;
            continue;
          }
          if (char === ',') {
            tokens.push({ type: TOKEN_TYPES.COMMA, value: ',' });
            i++;
            continue;
          }
          if (char === ':') {
            tokens.push({ type: TOKEN_TYPES.COLON, value: ':' });
            i++;
            continue;
          }
    
          // Comparison / Multi-char operators
          if (char === '<') {
            if (str[i + 1] === '>') {
              tokens.push({ type: TOKEN_TYPES.OPERATOR, value: '<>' });
              i += 2;
              continue;
            }
            if (str[i + 1] === '=') {
              tokens.push({ type: TOKEN_TYPES.OPERATOR, value: '<=' });
              i += 2;
              continue;
            }
            tokens.push({ type: TOKEN_TYPES.OPERATOR, value: '<' });
            i++;
            continue;
          }
          if (char === '>') {
            if (str[i + 1] === '=') {
              tokens.push({ type: TOKEN_TYPES.OPERATOR, value: '>=' });
              i += 2;
              continue;
            }
            tokens.push({ type: TOKEN_TYPES.OPERATOR, value: '>' });
            i++;
            continue;
          }
          if (char === '!' && str[i + 1] === '=') {
            tokens.push({ type: TOKEN_TYPES.OPERATOR, value: '!=' });
            i += 2;
            continue;
          }
          if (char === '=' || char === '+' || char === '-' || char === '*' || 
char === '/' || char === '^' || char === '%') {
            tokens.push({ type: TOKEN_TYPES.OPERATOR, value: char });
            i++;
            continue;
          }
    
          // Strings in quotes
          if (char === '"' || char === "'") {
            const quote = char;
            let s = '';
            i++;
            while (i < str.length && str[i] !== quote) {
              s += str[i];
              i++;
            }
            if (i < str.length && str[i] === quote) {
              i++;
            }
            tokens.push({ type: TOKEN_TYPES.STRING, value: s });
            continue;
          }
    
          // Numbers
          if (/[0-9]/.test(char) || (char === '.' && /[0-9]/.test(str[i + 1]))) 
{
            let numStr = '';
            while (i < str.length && /[0-9.]/.test(str[i])) {
              numStr += str[i];
              i++;
            }
            tokens.push({ type: TOKEN_TYPES.NUMBER, value: parseFloat(numStr) 
});
            continue;
          }
    
          // Identifiers, Cell references, Ranges, or Function names
          if (/[A-Za-z_\$]/.test(char)) {
            let ident = '';
            while (i < str.length && /[A-Za-z0-9_\$]/.test(str[i])) {
              ident += str[i];
              i++;
            }
    
            // Check if followed by colon for range: e.g. A1:B10
            if (str[i] === ':') {
              let j = i + 1;
              while (j < str.length && /\s/.test(str[j])) j++;
              if (/[A-Za-z0-9_\$]/.test(str[j])) {
                let ident2 = '';
                while (j < str.length && /[A-Za-z0-9_\$]/.test(str[j])) {
                  ident2 += str[j];
                  j++;
                }
                if (parseCellRef(ident) && parseCellRef(ident2)) {
                  tokens.push({ type: TOKEN_TYPES.RANGE_REF, value: 
`${ident}:${ident2}` });
                  i = j;
                  continue;
                }
              }
            }
    
            // Check if it's a cell reference
            if (parseCellRef(ident)) {
              tokens.push({ type: TOKEN_TYPES.CELL_REF, value: 
ident.toUpperCase() });
              continue;
            }
    
            // Otherwise identifier (function name like SUM, AVG, MIN, MAX, 
COUNT, or TRUE/FALSE)
            const upper = ident.toUpperCase();
            if (upper === 'TRUE') {
              tokens.push({ type: TOKEN_TYPES.NUMBER, value: 1 });
            } else if (upper === 'FALSE') {
              tokens.push({ type: TOKEN_TYPES.NUMBER, value: 0 });
            } else {
              tokens.push({ type: TOKEN_TYPES.IDENTIFIER, value: upper });
            }
            continue;
          }
    
          // Unknown character
          throw new Error(`Unexpected character: '${char}'`);
        }
    
        return tokens;
      }
    
      // --- Parser (Recursive Descent) ---
      class FormulaParser {
        constructor(tokens, cellLookupFn) {
          this.tokens = tokens;
          this.pos = 0;
          this.cellLookupFn = cellLookupFn || (() => 0);
        }
    
        peek() {
          return this.tokens[this.pos] || null;
        }
    
        consume(expectedType, expectedValue) {
          const token = this.peek();
          if (!token) throw new Error('#SYNTAX!');
          if (expectedType && token.type !== expectedType) {
            throw new Error('#SYNTAX!');
          }
          if (expectedValue && token.value !== expectedValue) {
            throw new Error('#SYNTAX!');
          }
          this.pos++;
          return token;
        }
    
        parse() {
          if (this.tokens.length === 0) return 0;
          const result = this.parseExpression();
          if (this.pos < this.tokens.length) {
            throw new Error('#SYNTAX!');
          }
          return result;
        }
    
        // Comparison: =, <>, !=, <, <=, >, >=
        parseExpression() {
          let left = this.parseAdditive();
    
          while (this.peek() && this.peek().type === TOKEN_TYPES.OPERATOR &&
            ['=', '<>', '!=', '<', '<=', '>', '>='].includes(this.peek().value))
{
            const op = this.consume().value;
            const right = this.parseAdditive();
            if (op === '=') left = (left === right) ? 1 : 0;
            else if (op === '<>' || op === '!=') left = (left !== right) ? 1 : 
0;
            else if (op === '<') left = (left < right) ? 1 : 0;
            else if (op === '<=') left = (left <= right) ? 1 : 0;
            else if (op === '>') left = (left > right) ? 1 : 0;
            else if (op === '>=') left = (left >= right) ? 1 : 0;
          }
    
          return left;
        }
    
        // Addition, Subtraction: +, -
        parseAdditive() {
          let left = this.parseMultiplicative();
    
          while (this.peek() && this.peek().type === TOKEN_TYPES.OPERATOR &&
            (this.peek().value === '+' || this.peek().value === '-')) {
            const op = this.consume().value;
            const right = this.parseMultiplicative();
            const lNum = this.toNumber(left);
            const rNum = this.toNumber(right);
            if (op === '+') left = lNum + rNum;
            else if (op === '-') left = lNum - rNum;
          }
    
          return left;
        }
    
        // Multiplication, Division: *, /
        parseMultiplicative() {
          let left = this.parseExponentiation();
    
          while (this.peek() && this.peek().type === TOKEN_TYPES.OPERATOR &&
            (this.peek().value === '*' || this.peek().value === '/')) {
            const op = this.consume().value;
            const right = this.parseExponentiation();
            const lNum = this.toNumber(left);
            const rNum = this.toNumber(right);
            if (op === '*') left = lNum * rNum;
            else if (op === '/') {
              if (rNum === 0) throw new Error('#DIV/0!');
              left = lNum / rNum;
            }
          }
    
          return left;
        }
    
        // Exponentiation: ^
        parseExponentiation() {
          let left = this.parseUnary();
    
          while (this.peek() && this.peek().type === TOKEN_TYPES.OPERATOR && 
this.peek().value === '^') {
            this.consume();
            const right = this.parseUnary();
            left = Math.pow(this.toNumber(left), this.toNumber(right));
          }
    
          return left;
        }
    
        // Unary: +, -
        parseUnary() {
          if (this.peek() && this.peek().type === TOKEN_TYPES.OPERATOR &&
            (this.peek().value === '+' || this.peek().value === '-')) {
            const op = this.consume().value;
            const operand = this.parseUnary();
            const num = this.toNumber(operand);
            return op === '-' ? -num : num;
          }
    
          return this.parsePrimary();
        }
    
        // Primary: Number, String, CellRef, FunctionCall, Range (in function), 
(Expr)
        parsePrimary() {
          const token = this.peek();
          if (!token) throw new Error('#SYNTAX!');
    
          if (token.type === TOKEN_TYPES.NUMBER) {
            this.consume();
            // Check for percent postfix
            if (this.peek() && this.peek().type === TOKEN_TYPES.OPERATOR && 
this.peek().value === '%') {
              this.consume();
              return token.value / 100;
            }
            return token.value;
          }
    
          if (token.type === TOKEN_TYPES.STRING) {
            this.consume();
            return token.value;
          }
    
          if (token.type === TOKEN_TYPES.CELL_REF) {
            this.consume();
            const cellVal = this.cellLookupFn(token.value);
            if (typeof cellVal === 'string' && cellVal.startsWith('#')) {
              throw new Error(cellVal);
            }
            return cellVal;
          }
    
          if (token.type === TOKEN_TYPES.IDENTIFIER) {
            return this.parseFunctionCall();
          }
    
          if (token.type === TOKEN_TYPES.LPAREN) {
            this.consume(TOKEN_TYPES.LPAREN);
            const expr = this.parseExpression();
            this.consume(TOKEN_TYPES.RPAREN);
            return expr;
          }
    
          throw new Error('#SYNTAX!');
        }
    
        parseFunctionCall() {
          const fnNameToken = this.consume(TOKEN_TYPES.IDENTIFIER);
          const fnName = fnNameToken.value.toUpperCase();
    
          this.consume(TOKEN_TYPES.LPAREN);
          const rawArgs = [];
    
          if (this.peek() && this.peek().type !== TOKEN_TYPES.RPAREN) {
            while (true) {
              // Check if argument is a RANGE_REF e.g. A1:B10
              if (this.peek() && this.peek().type === TOKEN_TYPES.RANGE_REF) {
                const rangeToken = this.consume(TOKEN_TYPES.RANGE_REF);
                const [r1, r2] = rangeToken.value.split(':');
                const cells = expandRange(r1, r2);
                for (const cellRef of cells) {
                  const val = this.cellLookupFn(cellRef);
                  rawArgs.push(val);
                }
              } else {
                // Expression argument
                const val = this.parseExpression();
                rawArgs.push(val);
              }
    
              if (this.peek() && this.peek().type === TOKEN_TYPES.COMMA) {
                this.consume(TOKEN_TYPES.COMMA);
              } else {
                break;
              }
            }
          }
    
          this.consume(TOKEN_TYPES.RPAREN);
          return this.evaluateFunction(fnName, rawArgs);
        }
    
        evaluateFunction(fnName, args) {
          // Flatten any nested arrays
          const flatArgs = [];
          const flatten = (items) => {
            for (const item of items) {
              if (Array.isArray(item)) flatten(item);
              else flatArgs.push(item);
            }
          };
          flatten(args);
    
          // Check for error propagation in arguments
          for (const arg of flatArgs) {
            if (typeof arg === 'string' && arg.startsWith('#')) {
              throw new Error(arg);
            }
          }
    
          // Filter numeric values for statistical functions
          const numbers = [];
          for (const val of flatArgs) {
            if (val === null || val === undefined || val === '') continue;
            if (typeof val === 'number') {
              if (!isNaN(val)) numbers.push(val);
            } else if (typeof val === 'string') {
              const num = Number(val);
              if (!isNaN(num) && val.trim() !== '') {
                numbers.push(num);
              }
            } else if (typeof val === 'boolean') {
              numbers.push(val ? 1 : 0);
            }
          }
    
          switch (fnName) {
            case 'SUM': {
              let sum = 0;
              for (const n of numbers) sum += n;
              return sum;
            }
            case 'AVG':
            case 'AVERAGE': {
              if (numbers.length === 0) throw new Error('#DIV/0!');
              let sum = 0;
              for (const n of numbers) sum += n;
              return sum / numbers.length;
            }
            case 'MIN': {
              if (numbers.length === 0) return 0;
              return Math.min(...numbers);
            }
            case 'MAX': {
              if (numbers.length === 0) return 0;
              return Math.max(...numbers);
            }
            case 'COUNT': {
              return numbers.length;
            }
            default:
              throw new Error('#NAME?');
          }
        }
    
        toNumber(val) {
          if (typeof val === 'number') return val;
          if (val === null || val === undefined || val === '') return 0;
          if (typeof val === 'boolean') return val ? 1 : 0;
          if (typeof val === 'string') {
            if (val.startsWith('#')) throw new Error(val);
            const num = Number(val);
            if (isNaN(num)) throw new Error('#VALUE!');
            return num;
          }
          return 0;
        }
      }
    
      // --- Evaluation Engine & Dependency Graph ---
      class WorkbookCalculator {
        constructor(cells = {}) {
          this.rawCells = { ...cells }; // cellRef -> string raw value
          this.computedValues = {};     // cellRef -> computed result (number, 
string, or error string)
          this.errors = {};             // cellRef -> error message
          this.dependencies = {};       // cellRef -> [deps]
          this.reverseDependencies = {};// dep -> [cells depending on it]
        }
    
        setCells(cells) {
          this.rawCells = { ...cells };
          this.computeAll();
        }
    
        setCell(ref, rawValue) {
          const normalizedRef = ref.toUpperCase();
          if (rawValue === '' || rawValue === null || rawValue === undefined) {
            delete this.rawCells[normalizedRef];
          } else {
            this.rawCells[normalizedRef] = String(rawValue);
          }
          this.computeAll();
        }
    
        computeAll() {
          this.computedValues = {};
          this.errors = {};
          this.dependencies = {};
          this.reverseDependencies = {};
    
          // 1. Build dependency graph
          for (const [ref, raw] of Object.entries(this.rawCells)) {
            if (typeof raw === 'string' && raw.startsWith('=')) {
              const deps = extractDependencies(raw);
              this.dependencies[ref] = deps;
              deps.forEach(d => {
                if (!this.reverseDependencies[d]) this.reverseDependencies[d] = 
[];
                this.reverseDependencies[d].push(ref);
              });
            }
          }
    
          // 2. Detect circular dependencies
          const circularCells = this._detectCycles();
          for (const circ of circularCells) {
            this.computedValues[circ] = '#CIRCULAR!';
            this.errors[circ] = 'Circular reference detected';
          }
    
          // 3. Evaluate non-circular cells in topological order
          const visited = new Set(circularCells);
          const evalCell = (ref) => {
            if (visited.has(ref)) return;
            visited.add(ref);
    
            const raw = this.rawCells[ref];
            if (raw === undefined || raw === null || raw === '') {
              this.computedValues[ref] = '';
              return;
            }
    
            if (typeof raw !== 'string' || !raw.startsWith('=')) {
              // Plain value: number or text
              const trimmed = String(raw).trim();
              const num = Number(trimmed);
              if (!isNaN(num) && trimmed !== '') {
                this.computedValues[ref] = num;
              } else {
                this.computedValues[ref] = raw;
              }
              return;
            }
    
            // Formula cell: evaluate dependencies first
            const deps = this.dependencies[ref] || [];
            for (const dep of deps) {
              if (!visited.has(dep) && !circularCells.has(dep)) {
                evalCell(dep);
              }
            }
    
            // If any direct dependency is a circular reference, propagate
            for (const dep of deps) {
              if (circularCells.has(dep)) {
                this.computedValues[ref] = '#CIRCULAR!';
                return;
              }
            }
    
            // Evaluate formula
            try {
              const tokens = tokenize(raw);
              const parser = new FormulaParser(tokens, (cellRef) => {
                const norm = cellRef.toUpperCase();
                if (circularCells.has(norm)) throw new Error('#CIRCULAR!');
                if (this.computedValues[norm] !== undefined) {
                  return this.computedValues[norm];
                }
                if (this.rawCells[norm] !== undefined) {
                  evalCell(norm);
                  return this.computedValues[norm] !== undefined ? 
this.computedValues[norm] : 0;
                }
                return 0; // Empty cell defaults to 0
              });
    
              const result = parser.parse();
              // Round floating point artifacts if needed (e.g. 0.1 + 0.2 -> 
0.3)
              if (typeof result === 'number' && isFinite(result)) {
                const rounded = Math.round(result * 1e10) / 1e10;
                this.computedValues[ref] = rounded;
              } else {
                this.computedValues[ref] = result;
              }
            } catch (err) {
              const errMsg = err.message.startsWith('#') ? err.message : 
'#ERROR!';
              this.computedValues[ref] = errMsg;
              this.errors[ref] = err.message;
            }
          };
    
          for (const ref of Object.keys(this.rawCells)) {
            evalCell(ref);
          }
    
          return this.computedValues;
        }
    
        _detectCycles() {
          const circular = new Set();
          const UNVISITED = 0, VISITING = 1, VISITED = 2;
          const state = {};
    
          const dfs = (node, path) => {
            state[node] = VISITING;
            path.push(node);
    
            const neighbors = this.dependencies[node] || [];
            for (const neighbor of neighbors) {
              if (this.rawCells[neighbor] && typeof this.rawCells[neighbor] === 
'string' && this.rawCells[neighbor].startsWith('=')) {
                if (state[neighbor] === VISITING) {
                  // Found cycle! Add all nodes in cycle path
                  const cycleStartIdx = path.indexOf(neighbor);
                  for (let i = cycleStartIdx; i < path.length; i++) {
                    circular.add(path[i]);
                  }
                } else if (state[neighbor] !== VISITED) {
                  dfs(neighbor, path);
                }
              }
            }
    
            path.pop();
            state[node] = VISITED;
          };
    
          for (const node of Object.keys(this.dependencies)) {
            if (!state[node]) {
              dfs(node, []);
            }
          }
    
          return circular;
        }
    
        getDisplayValue(ref) {
          const norm = ref.toUpperCase();
          if (this.computedValues[norm] !== undefined) {
            return this.computedValues[norm];
          }
          return this.rawCells[norm] !== undefined ? this.rawCells[norm] : '';
        }
    
        getRawValue(ref) {
          const norm = ref.toUpperCase();
          return this.rawCells[norm] !== undefined ? this.rawCells[norm] : '';
        }
      }
    
      // Known built-in functions with descriptions for autocomplete
      const SUPPORTED_FUNCTIONS = [
        { name: 'SUM', signature: 'SUM(value1, [value2, ...])', description: 
'Adds all the numbers in a range of cells' },
        { name: 'AVG', signature: 'AVG(value1, [value2, ...])', description: 
'Returns the average (arithmetic mean) of arguments' },
        { name: 'AVERAGE', signature: 'AVERAGE(value1, [value2, ...])', 
description: 'Returns the average of arguments' },
        { name: 'MIN', signature: 'MIN(value1, [value2, ...])', description: 
'Returns the smallest number in a set of values' },
        { name: 'MAX', signature: 'MAX(value1, [value2, ...])', description: 
'Returns the largest number in a set of values' },
        { name: 'COUNT', signature: 'COUNT(value1, [value2, ...])', description:
'Counts how many numbers are in the list of arguments' },
      ];
    
      return {
        colLetterToIndex,
        indexToColLetter,
        parseCellRef,
        expandRange,
        shiftCellRef,
        shiftFormula,
        extractDependencies,
        tokenize,
        FormulaParser,
        WorkbookCalculator,
        SUPPORTED_FUNCTIONS
      };
    });
